# Team LED colours — build 124 before/after list

For Tyler to review by eye on the spare controller only (never the home controller). Generated from the real tables:
the team rows come from `kTeamColors`, `TeamColorDatabase`, `NcaaConferences` and the commercial geo table
(the four tables `test/data/team_led_colors_test.dart` covers), the values from
`lib/data/team_led_colors.dart` at this commit. "Lights at" applies the fleet's colour gamma 2.8 the way the
controller does (`round(255·(v/255)^2.8)`): what the live view shows, before master brightness.

## 1. The rule, and the Chiefs values

The table keeps the rule it came with (fix/team-led-colors-2026-09-24, `8ee30c8`): hold the hue a fan names,
push saturation up, full value; `bri` owns brightness. Build 124 adds one rule for gold, from the bench of
2026-11-10 (brightness 128, gamma 2.8), where every lighter gold read **whiter**, not more golden:

- **Gold-family** = the entries the table already classes `gold→green-yellow` **and** whose brand hue is below
  50° (the usual gold/yellow boundary; Chiefs gold is 41.2°). The class's yellows (brand hue 50° and above,
  e.g. Belgium, BVB, Brazil, Oregon) are **not** changed: a yellow team must stay yellow. They are listed in
  section 3 for their own bench review.
- **Transform** for a gold-family entry: `LED' = (255, round(G × 168 / 181), 0)`. Red stays full, blue goes to
  0, green is scaled by the Chiefs move. Gamma is a power law, so scaling G scales the green **light** by the
  same (168/181)^2.8 ≈ 0.81 for every gold: each one moves as far, in light, as the Chiefs gold did.

**Chiefs, reproduced exactly:**

| Colour | Brand | Table before (8ee30c8) | Build 124 | Owner's pick | Lights at |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| Chiefs red | E31837 = 227,24,55 | 255,0,34 | 255,0,34 | 255,0,34 | 255,0,1 |
| Chiefs gold | FFB81C = 255,184,28 | 255,181,20 | 255,168,0 | 255,168,0 | 255,79,0 |

Gold: round(181 × 168 / 181) = 168, blue 20 → 0. Red is not gold-family; its entry already was 255,0,34.

## 4. The server mirror

`functions/src/teamLedColors.ts` must carry exactly these values. It is generated from the Dart table by
`dart run scripts/gen_team_led_mirror.dart` (never edited by hand; `--check` exits 1 on drift), and the
**drift test exists**: `test/data/team_led_colors_test.dart`, group "server mirror", parses the `.ts` and
fails unless it is entry-for-entry identical. Its own jest tests are `functions/test/unit/teamLedColors.test.js`.
In build 124 **no deployed function imports it**: wiring the Game Day planner to it (`b9eac14` on
fix/team-led-colors-2026-09-24) is a separate, gated functions deploy.

## 5. Every team colour sent differently from its brand value (2354 of 2635 team slots)

Before = the brand colour (what a screen shows, and what controllers were sent before the table); after = the
LED colour build 124 sends. A slot whose LED value equals its brand value is not listed.
